[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI1);
[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    /* 3. SPE = 1 by hand (the J1 pins are in input in this step) */
[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    k = c;

[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);

[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    /* 4. AFCNTR = 1 in force: another CPOL or SSIOP */

[+   1.763s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i4 = SPI_BM_Init(&k);

[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);

[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    /* the control: a valid Init changes the snapshot */
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);

[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t w = SPI_BM_Init(&k);
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    const bool ctrl = !snap_eq(a, b);

[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T2 on SPI1: register images against sst_t2_expect() ------------------------------- */
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_t2(void)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = SST_AREA->m4_res;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t bits_list[4] = { 4U, 8U, 16U, 32U };
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad_d = 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad_r = 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(SPI1);
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI1);
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mbr = 0U; mbr <= 7U; mbr++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode <= 3U; mode++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < 4U; ib++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t bits = bits_list[ib];
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t thr[2] = { 1U, thr_max(bits) };
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t it = 0U; it < 2U; it++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t lsb = 0U; lsb < 2U; lsb++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                        for (uint32_t dir = 0U; dir < 2U; dir++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                            for (uint32_t nss = 0U; nss < 3U; nss++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                            {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                for (uint32_t op = 0U; op < 2U; op++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                    for (uint32_t mi = 0U; mi < 4U; mi++)
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                    {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        const uint32_t mssi = ((mi & 1U) != 0U) ? 15U : 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        const uint32_t midi = ((mi & 2U) != 0U) ? 15U : 0U;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        if ((nss == SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT) && (mssi != 0U))
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        {
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                            continue;
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        }
[+   1.787s] [host] diff| +                                        for (uint32_t keep = 0U; keep < 2U; keep++)
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                        {
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI1, mbr, mode, bits, lsb != 0U, dir, thr[it],
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                                       nss, op != 0U, mssi, midi, keep != 0U };
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            uint32_t e1 = 0U;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            uint32_t e2 = 0U;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            uint32_t ecr = 0U;

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            const uint32_t g2 = SPI1->CFG2;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (SPI1->CFG1 != e1) || (g2 != e2) ||

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                bad++;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            }

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                (SPI1->UDRDR != 0U))
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            {
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                bad_r++;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            }

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            {
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                                bad_d++;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                            n++;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                        }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                    }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                                }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                            }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t e1 = 0U;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t e2 = 0U;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ecr = 0U;
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c0 = mkcfg(0U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    sst_t2_expect(&c0, &e1, &e2, &ecr);
[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c0);

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI1);

[+   1.791s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = bad;

[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +/* ---- J1 pins of SPI1 (AF5): the claim reads SPI1 back at rest first (S14, R2-3) ------- */

[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_j1pins(uint32_t what, uint32_t cpol, uint32_t speed)
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = SST_AREA->m4_res;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(SPI1);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI1);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    bool ok;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    if (what == SST_J1P_RELEASE)
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_SCK, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MOSI, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        s_af_speed = speed;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        ok = j1_af(PA_J1_SCK, GPIOA, (cpol != 0U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +             j1_af(PB_J1_MOSI, GPIOB, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        /* one MISO pin in AF at a time: the other goes to input first */
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        if (ok && (what == SST_J1P_LOOP))
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +            ok = pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +                 j1_af(PA_J1_MISO, GPIOA, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        else if (ok)
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +            ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +                 j1_af(PB_J1_MISO, GPIOB, (what == SST_J1P_PB4_UP) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        s_af_speed = GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    r[0] = ok ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = ((GPIOA->MODER >> (PA_J1_SCK * 2U)) & 0xFUL) | (((GPIOB->MODER >> (PB_J1_MISO * 2U)) & 0xFUL) << 4);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +/* ---- Transfers on SPI1: polling, interrupt, DMA2 ----------------------------------- */
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Xfer_t      s_it;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static volatile bool     s_it_active;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_irqs;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_spurious;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t          s_b1_irq_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t          s_b1_irq_max;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +/* The project's vector for the test (plan C14), CPU2's NVIC, priority 6. */
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +void SPI1_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    s_it_irqs++;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    if (s_it_active)
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        const bool done = SPI_BM_TransferIrq(SPI1, &s_it);
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.794s] [host] diff| +        if (dt < s_b1_irq_min)
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_min = dt;
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        if (dt > s_b1_irq_max)
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_max = dt;
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        if (done)
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        s_it_spurious++;
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +        SPI1->IER = 0U;
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.795s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +static bool m4_stream(DMA_Stream_TypeDef *s, bool tx, uint32_t es, void *buf, uint32_t n)
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t req = 0U;

[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    const DmaBm_ConfigEx_t d = {
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +        .base = { .stream = s, .request_id = req, .direction = tx ? DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH : DMA_BM_DIR_PERIPH_TO_MEM,
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_data_size = sz, .mem_data_size = sz, .periph_inc = false, .mem_inc = true,
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +                  .circular = false, .priority = DMA_BM_PRIORITY_HIGH, .enable_it_ht = false, .enable_it_tc = false,

[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_addr = tx ? (uint32_t)&SPI1->TXDR : (uint32_t)&SPI1->RXDR, .mem_addr = (uint32_t)buf,
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +                  .buffer_length = n },
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +        .mem1_addr = 0U, .mem_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE,
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +        .periph_flow_ctrl = false, .fail_if_busy = true, .stop_spin_limit = 0U };
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    return DMA_BM_InitStreamEx(&d) && DMA_BM_StartEx(s);
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_xfer(uint32_t xc, uint32_t seed, uint32_t n, uint32_t xm)
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = SST_AREA->m4_res;
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bits = SST_XCFG_BITS(xc);
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = SST_XCFG_MBR(xc);
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    if ((n == 0U) || (n > M4_MAXF))
[+   1.796s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.799s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = (uint32_t)SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.799s] [host] diff| +        return;

[+   1.837s] [host] diff| +/* Bit k set = register k differs from its reset value (T14's oracle). */
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t reset_diff(const SPI_TypeDef *s)
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[NREG];
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(s, r);
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t d = 0U;
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < NREG; k++)
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +        if (r[k] != k_reset_regs[k])
[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            d |= 1UL << k;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    return d;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +/* T14 for one instance: Deinit, then the reset image. The control (`ctrl`) runs the
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +   same comparison on the instance still initialised, which must differ. */
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +static void t14_check(const char *tag, SPI_TypeDef *spi, const SpiBm_Config_t *ctrl)
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Init(ctrl);
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        dctrl = reset_diff(spi);
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d = reset_diff(spi);
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st == SPI_BM_OK) && (d == 0U) && ((ctrl == NULL) || (dctrl != 0U)),
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            "T14 %s Deinit %u, registers that differ from the RM0399 reset image 0x%02lX (0); control, still "
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            "initialised: 0x%02lX%s", tag, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)d, (unsigned long)dctrl,
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            (ctrl == NULL) ? " (no control)" : " (!= 0)");
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T10: the kernel clock field, against RCC read directly ---------------------------- */
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t10(void)
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[6] = { SPI1, SPI2, SPI3, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d2 = RCC->D2CCIP1R;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d3 = RCC->D3CCIPR;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    /* Field positions typed from RM0399 9.7.19 (SPI123SEL 14:12, SPI45SEL 18:16) and
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +       9.7.21 (SPI6SEL 30:28). */
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t raw[6] = { (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 12) & 7U, (d2 >> 16) & 7U,
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +                              (d2 >> 16) & 7U, (d3 >> 28) & 7U };
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t got[6] = { 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U, 9U };
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 6U; i++)
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        if ((SPI_BM_GetKernelSource(inst[i], &got[i]) != SPI_BM_OK) || (got[i] != raw[i]) || (got[i] != 0U))
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T10 GetKernelSource SPI1..6 = %lu %lu %lu %lu %lu %lu; RCC_D2CCIP1R 0x%08lX RCC_D3CCIPR 0x%08lX "
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            "read directly give the same (0 in prj_01): differ 0x%02lX", (unsigned long)got[0], (unsigned long)got[1],
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)got[2], (unsigned long)got[3], (unsigned long)got[4], (unsigned long)got[5],
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)d2, (unsigned long)d3, (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.842s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T3: the pure functions, printed for the host to recompute (C15) ------------------- */
[+   1.847s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_calc_max;     /* B1: worst CalcPrescaler, cycles */
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +static void t3_p(uint32_t *i, uint32_t k, uint32_t x)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0xFFU;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(k, x, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    if (dt > s_b1_calc_max)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        s_b1_calc_max = dt;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3P %lu k=%lu x=%lu st=%u mbr=%lu sck=%lu", (unsigned long)*i, (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)x,
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? mbr : 0U), (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? sck : 0U));
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    (*i)++;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +static void t3_g(uint32_t *i, uint32_t c, uint32_t k, uint32_t m)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t cyc = 0U;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_CalcGuardCycles(c, k, m, &cyc);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3G %lu c=%lu k=%lu m=%lu st=%u cyc=%lu", (unsigned long)*i, (unsigned long)c, (unsigned long)k,
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)m, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)((st == SPI_BM_OK) ? cyc : 0U));
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    (*i)++;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t3(void)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ks[] = { 1U, 2U, 3U, 255U, 256U, 257U, 511U, 512U, 513U, 1000000U, 99999989U,
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +                                   100000000U, 101250000U, 200000000U, 202600000U, 268435456U, 2147483647U,
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +                                   2147483648U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < (sizeof(ks) / sizeof(ks[0])); a++)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t k = ks[a];
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        /* the limits of the rule: max = k/2, k/256 (exact, and one below), their
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +           neighbours, 1, k itself, k + 1, the bench speeds and a small prime */
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t xs[11] = { k / 2U, (k / 2U) - 1U, k / 256U, (k / 256U) - 1U, (k / 256U) + 1U, 1U, k,
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +                                  (k == 0xFFFFFFFFUL) ? k : (k + 1U), 25000000U, 12500000U, 7U };
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 11U; b++)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +            if (xs[b] != 0U)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +                t3_p(&n, k, xs[b]);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 0U, 1U);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 1U, 0U);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    t3_p(&n, 0U, 0U);
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t np = n;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t cs[] = { 1U, 64000000U, 200000000U, 400000000U, 405200000U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t kk[] = { 1U, 3U, 100000000U, 200000000U, 4294967295U };
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t g = 0U;
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < (sizeof(cs) / sizeof(cs[0])); a++)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < (sizeof(kk) / sizeof(kk[0])); b++)
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.848s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 0U);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 3U);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +            t3_g(&g, cs[a], kk[b], 7U);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m <= 8U; m++)
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +        t3_g(&g, 400000000U, 100000000U, m);       /* the bench's SPI4-6; m = 8 refused */
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 0U, 1U, 0U);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 1U, 0U, 0U);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    t3_g(&g, 4294967295U, 1U, 7U);                 /* 2^40 cycles: ERR_RANGE */
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3 done: %lu CalcPrescaler vectors, %lu CalcGuardCycles vectors (the host recomputes them)",
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)np, (unsigned long)g);
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T1: refusals, with the registers of the instances and both RCC banks read
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +   before and after (C6, C12). The expected status of each case is written by hand. ---- */
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +#define T1_MAX        64U
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +static uint8_t  s_t1_got[T1_MAX];

[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +static void t1(SpiBm_Status_t got, SpiBm_Status_t exp)
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    if (s_t1_n < T1_MAX)
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +        s_t1_got[s_t1_n] = (uint8_t)got;
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +        s_t1_exp[s_t1_n] = (uint8_t)exp;
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    s_t1_n++;
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +#define T1_SNAP       ((4U * NREG) + 6U)

[+   1.851s] [host] diff| +    {
