[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T9: the engine, without pins: t(2000) - t(1000) in CPU cycles ------------------ */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +static void t9_one(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, uint32_t mbr, uint32_t bits, uint32_t *bad)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t[2] = { 0U, 0U };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Status_t st[2] = { SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM, SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_tx, sst_elem_bytes(bits), 2000U, bits, next_seed());
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 2U; k++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t n = (k + 1U) * 1000U;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        st[k] = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, n, guard_of(spi, mbr), budget_of(spi, mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        t[k] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    /* Oracle: 1000 frames x DSIZE x 2^(MBR+1) kernel ticks, in CPU cycles. */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const uint64_t num = 1000ULL * bits * (1ULL << (mbr + 1U)) * s_cpu_hz;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const double exp_c = (double)num / (double)kernel_of(spi);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const double ctl_c = exp_c * 2.0;            /* the same count with MBR + 1 */
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const double d = (double)(t[1] - t[0]);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const double err = (d - exp_c) / exp_c;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const double cerr = (d - ctl_c) / ctl_c;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const bool ok = (st[0] == SPI_BM_OK) && (st[1] == SPI_BM_OK) && (err <= 0.01) && (err >= -0.01) &&
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +                    ((cerr > 0.01) || (cerr < -0.01));
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        (*bad)++;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok, "T9 %s TX only MBR /%lu %lu bits: t(2000) - t(1000) = %lu cycles, expected %lu (%+ld ppm, +-1 %%); "
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            "with MBR + 1 %lu (%+ld ppm, must be out); st %u %u", name, (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr),
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)(t[1] - t[0]), (unsigned long)exp_c, (long)(err * 1e6),
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)ctl_c, (long)(cerr * 1e6), (unsigned)st[0], (unsigned)st[1]);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t9(void)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[3] = { SPI2, SPI3, SPI5 };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    const char *const names[3] = { "SPI2", "SPI3", "SPI5" };
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 3U; i++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(inst[i]);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < 2U; m++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +                t9_one(inst[i], names[i], (m == 0U) ? 3U : 5U, (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U, &bad);
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +    (void)bad;
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.865s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T11: mode fault on SPI2 (no pin, plan R2-4), and TakeErrors -------------------- */
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t11(void)
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = SPI2;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t g = guard_of(spi, 7U);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bud = budget_of(spi, 7U, 8U, 4U);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_tx, 1U, 4U, 8U, next_seed());
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    /* control first: SSI inactive, no fault */
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_ctrl = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U, g, bud);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t modf_ctrl = spi->SR & SPI_SR_MODF;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    /* SSI made active by hand (SSIOP = 0, SSM = 1): mode fault, with SPE = 0 already
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +       (plan revision 2, P1) */
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SSI;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    __DSB();
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_f = spi->SR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_f = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U, g, bud);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cr1_f = spi->CR1;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cfg2_f = spi->CFG2;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_after = spi->SR;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t d1 = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i1 = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s_rec = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 4U, g, bud);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    verdict((s_ctrl == SPI_BM_OK) && (modf_ctrl == 0U), "T11(a) control: SSI inactive, Transfer %u, MODF %lu (0)",
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)s_ctrl, (unsigned long)(modf_ctrl != 0U));
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    verdict(((sr_f & SPI_SR_MODF) != 0U) && (s_f == SPI_BM_ERR_MODF) && ((cr1_f & SPI_CR1_SPE) == 0U) &&
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            ((cfg2_f & SPI_CFG2_MASTER) == 0U) && ((sr_after & SPI_SR_MODF) == 0U),
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            "T11(a) SSI active by hand: SR 0x%08lX (MODF), Transfer %u (ERR_MODF %u), then SPE %lu MASTER %lu, MODF "
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            "cleared %u", (unsigned long)sr_f, (unsigned)s_f, (unsigned)SPI_BM_ERR_MODF,
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((cr1_f & SPI_CR1_SPE) != 0U), (unsigned long)((cfg2_f & SPI_CFG2_MASTER) != 0U),
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)((sr_after & SPI_SR_MODF) == 0U));
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    verdict((d1 == SPI_BM_OK) && (i1 == SPI_BM_OK) && (s_rec == SPI_BM_OK),
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            "T11(a) Deinit %u + Init %u recover: Transfer %u", (unsigned)d1, (unsigned)i1, (unsigned)s_rec);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    /* (b) OVR by hand: full duplex, 32 frames pushed, the RxFIFO (16) never read. */
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t f = mkcfg(spi, 2U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&f);
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR2 = 32U;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pushed = 0U;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_CSTART;
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    while (((spi->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (10U * CYC_PER_MS)))
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +        if ((pushed < 32U) && ((spi->SR & SPI_SR_TXP) != 0U))
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.866s] [host] diff| +            *(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)spi + 0x20U) = (uint8_t)pushed;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +            pushed++;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_b = spi->SR;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t taken = SPI_BM_TakeErrors(spi);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t sr_a = spi->SR;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    spi->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    verdict(((sr_b & SPI_SR_OVR) != 0U) && (taken == (sr_b & (SPI_SR_OVR | SPI_SR_MODF | SPI_SR_TIFRE))) &&
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +            ((sr_a & (SPI_SR_OVR | SPI_SR_MODF | SPI_SR_TIFRE)) == 0U),
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +            "T11(b) OVR by hand (32 frames, RxFIFO unread): SR 0x%08lX, TakeErrors 0x%03lX, then SR 0x%08lX: "
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +            "taken = the error flags set, all cleared", (unsigned long)sr_b, (unsigned long)taken, (unsigned long)sr_a);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    t14_check("SPI2", spi, &c);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T13: RCC banks. SPI2 transmits from DMA1 stream 1 (circular, 64 B) for 65535
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +   frames at /256 while CPU2 disables its own bank, then CPU1 disables its bank. ---- */
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +#define T13_STREAM    DMA1_Stream1
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t ctsize(const SPI_TypeDef *spi)
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    return (spi->SR >> SPI_SR_CTSIZE_Pos) & 0xFFFFU;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t13(void)
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = SPI2;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    SstArea_t *a = SST_AREA;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t req = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_GetDmaRequest(spi, true, &req);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_dma1_buf, 1U, 64U, 8U, next_seed());
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t p = SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(spi, 65535U, false);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const DmaBm_ConfigEx_t d = {
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        .base = { .stream = T13_STREAM, .request_id = req, .direction = DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE, .mem_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_inc = false, .mem_inc = true, .circular = true, .priority = DMA_BM_PRIORITY_HIGH,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .enable_it_ht = false, .enable_it_tc = false, .enable_it_te = false, .enable_it_dme = false,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .enable_it_fe = false, .enable_trbuff = false, .enable_fifo = false,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .fifo_threshold = DMA_BM_FIFO_TH_1_2, .periph_addr = (uint32_t)&spi->TXDR,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +                  .mem_addr = (uint32_t)s_dma1_buf, .buffer_length = 64U },
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        .mem1_addr = 0U, .mem_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE,
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +        .periph_flow_ctrl = false, .fail_if_busy = true, .stop_spin_limit = 0U };
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const bool dmaok = DMA_BM_InitStreamEx(&d) && DMA_BM_StartEx(T13_STREAM);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s = SPI_BM_DmaStart(spi);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(20U);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c0 = ctsize(spi);
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t c1 = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t c2 = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r1 = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r2 = 0U;
[+   1.868s] [host] diff| +    bool m1 = false;
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    bool m2 = false;
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T13, 1U))
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +        r1 = a->m4_res[2];
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +        m1 = (a->m4_res[0] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(30U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    c1 = ctsize(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T13, 2U))
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +        r2 = a->m4_res[2];
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +        m2 = (a->m4_res[0] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) && (a->m4_res[1] == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(30U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    c2 = ctsize(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c1bank_on = RCC_C1->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN;
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    /* CPU1 disables its bank: nobody enables SPI2 now; 30 ms later its count must not
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +       have moved (read again once the clock is back: an unclocked SPI reads nothing). */
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t dc = SPI_BM_DisableClock(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t both_off = (RCC_C1->APB1LENR | RCC_C2->APB1LENR) & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN;
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(30U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c3 = ctsize(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_BM_StopEx(T13_STREAM, 0U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_DmaEnd(spi, guard_of(spi, 7U));
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_BM_DeinitStream(T13_STREAM);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const bool run1 = (c0 > c1) && ((c0 - c1) > 1000U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const bool run2 = (c1 > c2) && ((c1 - c2) > 1000U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    const bool stop = (c2 >= c3) && ((c2 - c3) < 200U);
[+   1.869s] [host] diff| +    verdict((p == SPI_BM_OK) && dmaok && (s == SPI_BM_OK) && m1 && run1 && ((r1 & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN) == 0U),
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "T13 SPI2 sending by DMA1 S1: CTSIZE %lu; CPU2 DisableClock(SPI2) (RCC_C2 APB1LENR SPI2EN %lu): 30 ms later "
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "%lu, still running", (unsigned long)c0, (unsigned long)((r1 & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN) != 0U),
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)c1);
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +    verdict(m2 && run2 && ((r2 & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN) == 0U) && (c1bank_on != 0U),
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "T13 CPU2 EnableClock + DisableClock(SPI2): RCC_C2 SPI2EN %lu, RCC_C1 SPI2EN %lu; 30 ms later CTSIZE %lu, "
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "still running", (unsigned long)((r2 & RCC_APB1LENR_SPI2EN) != 0U), (unsigned long)(c1bank_on != 0U),
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)c2);
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +    verdict((dc == SPI_BM_OK) && (both_off == 0U) && stop,
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "T13 control: CPU1 DisableClock(SPI2) %u, both banks off; 30 ms later (clock back) CTSIZE %lu, %lu before: "
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +            "stopped", (unsigned)dc, (unsigned long)c3, (unsigned long)c2);
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +/* ---- CPU2's no-pin tests (T1, T2, T9 on SPI1), printed by CPU1 ----------------------- */
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +static void f1_m4_nopin(void)
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +    SstArea_t *a = SST_AREA;
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T1, 0U))
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +        const volatile uint32_t *r = a->m4_res;
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +        verdict((r[1] == 0U) && (r[2] == 0xFU) && (r[3] == 1U),
[+   1.870s] [host] diff| +                "T1 CM4 refusals on SPI1: %lu cases, %lu with another status (first %lu: %lu, expected %lu); registers "
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                "and RCC banks unchanged in the groups 0x%lX (0xF); control changes them %lu", (unsigned long)r[0],
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r[1], (unsigned long)r[4], (unsigned long)r[5], (unsigned long)r[6],
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r[2], (unsigned long)r[3]);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(SST_CMD_T2, 0U))
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +        const volatile uint32_t *r = a->m4_res;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +        verdict((r[1] == 0U) && (r[2] == 0U) && (r[3] == 0U) && (r[4] == 1U),
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                "T2 CM4 SPI1 %lu configurations against the RM0399 field table: differ %lu, Deinit failed %lu, "
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                "I2SCFGR/CRCPOLY/UDRDR off reset %lu; control caught %lu (first difference: config %lu CFG2 0x%08lX/0x%08lX)",
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r[0], (unsigned long)r[1], (unsigned long)r[2], (unsigned long)r[3], (unsigned long)r[4],
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r[5], (unsigned long)r[6], (unsigned long)r[7]);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < 2U; m++)
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t mbr = (m == 0U) ? 3U : 5U;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +            if (cm4_send4(SST_CMD_T9, mbr, bits, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t d = a->m4_res[1] - a->m4_res[0];
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                /* CPU2 at 200 MHz, SPI1's kernel at 200 MHz: x1 (plan T9) */
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                const double exp_c = 1000.0 * (double)bits * (double)(2UL << mbr) * ((double)a->m4_res[2] / (double)s_k123);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                const double err = ((double)d - exp_c) / exp_c;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                const double cerr = ((double)d - (2.0 * exp_c)) / (2.0 * exp_c);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                verdict((a->m4_res[3] == 0U) && (err <= 0.01) && (err >= -0.01) && ((cerr > 0.01) || (cerr < -0.01)),
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                        "T9 SPI1 (CM4 at %lu Hz) TX only MBR /%lu %lu bits: t(2000) - t(1000) = %lu cycles, expected %lu "
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                        "(%+ld ppm, +-1 %%); with MBR + 1 %+ld ppm (must be out); status 0x%lX", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[2],
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                        (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr), (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)d, (unsigned long)exp_c,
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +                        (long)(err * 1e6), (long)(cerr * 1e6), (unsigned long)a->m4_res[3]);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +/* ---- B1 (CPU1): cycles of a cold Init and of a 1-frame Transfer on SPI2 ------------ */
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_irq_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_irq_max;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +static void f1_b1(void)
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = SPI2;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, 0U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    s_tx[0] = 0x5AU;
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.871s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c_init = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, s_tx, NULL, 1U, guard_of(spi, 0U), budget_of(spi, 0U, 8U, 1U));
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c_xfer = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    LOG("B1 CM7 SPI_BM_Init %lu cycles (st %u), SPI_BM_Transfer of 1 frame at /2 %lu cycles (st %u), "
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +        "CalcPrescaler worst %lu", (unsigned long)c_init, (unsigned)si, (unsigned long)c_xfer, (unsigned)st,
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)s_b1_calc_max);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +/* The F1_NOPIN step: no pin moves, the host waits for the closing handshake. */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +static void step_f1_nopin(void)
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("F1_NOPIN"))
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t10();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t3();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t1();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t2_caps();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI2, "SPI2");
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI3, "SPI3");
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI4, "SPI4");
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI5, "SPI5");
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    t2_inst(SPI6, "SPI6");
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t7d();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t9();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t11();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_t13();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_m4_nopin();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    f1_b1();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[5] = { SPI2, SPI3, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    const char *const names[5] = { "SPI2", "SPI3", "SPI4", "SPI5", "SPI6" };
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 5U; i++)
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +        t14_check(names[i], inst[i], NULL);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    if (!sync0())
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| + * F1 on J1: SPI6 (CPU1, AF8) and SPI1 (CPU2, AF5), the wire PB5 -> PA6 as the oracle
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +static const char k_ord[2] = { 'M', 'L' };
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +/* The four J1 pins for SPI6 (S14: SPI6 read back at rest before each AF). SCK pulled
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +   to CPOL (plan "Archivos": pull of SCK = CPOL), MOSI and MISO without pull; `miso`
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +   0 = PA6 (the loop), 1/2 = PB4 with pull-up/down and PA6 in input (plan D2). The
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +   one MISO pin in AF is always switched off before the other is switched on. */
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +static bool spi6_pins(uint32_t cpol, uint32_t miso, uint32_t speed)
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = speed;
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_SCK, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, (cpol != 0U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U) &&
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +              pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MOSI, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.872s] [host] diff| +    if (ok && (miso == 0U))
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +        ok = pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U) &&
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    else if (ok)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +        ok = pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U) &&
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +             pin_cfg(GPIOB, PB_J1_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI6, (miso == 1U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +static bool j1_release(void)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    return j1_inputs(GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +/* One polling transfer on a CPU1 instance: pattern, Init, Transfer, Deinit (T14 per
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +   transfer), and what the oracles need. The RX buffer starts with a sentinel. */
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +static void xfer_local(const SpiBm_Config_t *c, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, void *tx, void *rx, SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = c->spi;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bits = c->data_bits;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill_const(rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->init_st = SPI_BM_Init(c);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->status = SPI_BM_Transfer(spi, tx, rx, n, guard_of(spi, c->mbr), budget_of(spi, c->mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->cycles = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->mism = sst_mismatch(tx, rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->crc = sst_crc32_frames(rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->cov = sst_coverage(tx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->irqs = 0U;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->sentinel_left = 0U;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->deinit_st = SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +/* One transfer on SPI1, run by CPU2 (SST_CMD_XFER). */
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +static bool xfer_m4(uint32_t xcfg, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, uint32_t xm, SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_XFER, xcfg, seed, n, xm))
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    const volatile uint32_t *s = SST_AREA->m4_res;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->status = s[0];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->mism = s[1];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->crc = s[2];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->cov = s[3];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->cycles = s[4];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->irqs = s[5];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->init_st = s[6];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->deinit_st = s[7];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    r->sentinel_left = s[8];
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +static bool xfer_ok(const SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +    return (r->status == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) && (r->mism == 0U) && (r->init_st == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +           (r->deinit_st == (uint32_t)SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.874s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +static void t4_line(const char *name, uint32_t mode, uint32_t bits, uint32_t lsb, uint32_t thr, uint32_t n,
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                    uint32_t seed, const SstXferRes_t *r)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T4 %s m%lu b%lu %c t%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX cov=%08lX cyc=%lu", name, (unsigned long)mode,
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)bits, k_ord[lsb], (unsigned long)thr, (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)seed,
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)r->status, (unsigned long)r->mism, (unsigned long)r->crc, (unsigned long)r->cov,
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)r->cycles);
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +/* T4: the matrix of the plan on one instance. `m4` = SPI1 through CPU2. */
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +static void t4_matrix(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, const uint32_t *bl, uint32_t nb)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0U;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(kernel_of(spi), 12500000U, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t count = 0U;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t worst = 0U;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode <= 3U; mode++)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, mode >> 1, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U) || (SST_AREA->m4_res[0] != 1U))
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                bench_abort("CPU2 J1 pins");
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        else if (!spi6_pins(mode >> 1, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH))
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < nb; ib++)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = bl[ib];
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t f = fifo_frames(spi, bits);
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t frames[8] = { 1U, 2U, 3U, f - 1U, f, f + 1U, 255U, 4096U };
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t thr[2] = { 1U, thr_max(spi, bits) };
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t lsb = 0U; lsb < 2U; lsb++)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t it = 0U; it < 2U; it++)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 8U; k++)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        if (m4)
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                            if (!xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(mode, bits, lsb, thr[it], mbr, 0U), frames[k], seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r))
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                            {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                                bench_abort("CPU2 transfer");
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                                return;
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                            }
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        else
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, mode, bits, lsb != 0U, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, thr[it]);
[+   1.877s] [host] diff| +                            xfer_local(&c, frames[k], seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                        t4_line(name, mode, bits, lsb, thr[it], frames[k], seed, &r);
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                        count++;
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                        if (!xfer_ok(&r))
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.882s] [host] diff| +                            bad++;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                        if (r.cycles > worst)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                            worst = r.cycles;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (count > 0U), "T4 %s loop through J1 at /%lu (%lu Hz): %lu transfers, %lu with a status, a "
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            "mismatch or a failed Init/Deinit; longest %lu cycles (every one within its max_cycles)", name,
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr), (unsigned long)sck, (unsigned long)count, (unsigned long)bad,
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)worst);
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +/* T4's control (plan D2): MISO moved to PB4, pulled up then down: RX all ones / all
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +   zeros. The host computes the mismatches from the seed. */
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +static void t4_control(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, const uint32_t *bl, uint32_t nb)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mbr = 0U;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sck = 0U;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_CalcPrescaler(kernel_of(spi), 12500000U, &mbr, &sck);
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = 0U; p < 2U; p++)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            if (!cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, (p == 0U) ? SST_J1P_PB4_UP : SST_J1P_PB4_DOWN, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U) ||
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                (SST_AREA->m4_res[0] != 1U))
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                bench_abort("CPU2 J1 pins");
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        else if (!spi6_pins(0U, p + 1U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH))
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < nb; ib++)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = bl[ib];
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            if (m4)
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, bits, 0U, 1U, mbr, 0U), 255U, seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            else
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                xfer_local(&c, 255U, seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T4C %s b%lu %s n255 s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX", name, (unsigned long)bits,
[+   1.883s] [host] diff| +                (p == 0U) ? "up" : "down", (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)r.status, (unsigned long)r.mism,
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r.crc);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T6: transfers by interrupt (SPI6 here; SPI1's ISR is CPU2's) ------------------- */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Xfer_t      s_it;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static volatile bool     s_it_active;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_irqs;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_it_spurious;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +/* The project's vector for the test (plan C14: not the driver's), priority 6. A call
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +   with no transfer running is the T6(c) control's spurious IRQ: counted, IER cleared. */
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +void SPI6_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    s_it_irqs++;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    if (s_it_active)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        const bool done = SPI_BM_TransferIrq(SPI6, &s_it);
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        if (dt < s_b1_irq_min)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_min = dt;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        if (dt > s_b1_irq_max)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +            s_b1_irq_max = dt;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        if (done)
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        s_it_spurious++;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +        SPI6->IER = 0U;
[+   1.887s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +static void t6_spi6(void)
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    IRQn_Type irq = SPI6_IRQn;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_GetIRQn(SPI6, &irq);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(irq, 6U, 0U);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 4U;                      /* /32: 3.125 MHz */
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t after_total = 0U;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t frames[3] = { 1U, fifo_frames(SPI6, bits) + 1U, 1024U };
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 3U; k++)
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t n = frames[k];
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            sst_fill(s_tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            sst_fill_const(s_rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            s_it.tx = s_tx;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            s_it.rx = s_rx;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            s_it.frames = n;
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            s_it.guard_cycles = guard_of(SPI6, mbr);
[+   1.888s] [host] diff| +            s_it_irqs = 0U;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            s_it_spurious = 0U;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = true;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t ss = SPI_BM_TransferStartIt(SPI6, &s_it);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            if (ss != SPI_BM_OK)
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +                s_it_active = false;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bud = budget_of(SPI6, mbr, bits, n);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            while (s_it_active && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < bud))
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const bool done = !s_it_active;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t irqs = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            /* (c) ES0445 2.22.3: 10 ms after the end, SPE = 0, no IRQ of SPI6 */
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            delay_ms(10U);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t after = s_it_irqs - irqs;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t spe = SPI6->CR1 & SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            after_total += after;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t mism = sst_mismatch(s_tx, s_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t crc = sst_crc32_frames(s_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T6 SPI6 b%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX irq=%lu after=%lu", (unsigned long)bits,
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)s_it.status, (unsigned long)mism,
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)crc, (unsigned long)irqs, (unsigned long)after);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            if ((si != SPI_BM_OK) || (ss != SPI_BM_OK) || !done || (s_it.status != SPI_BM_OK) || (mism != 0U) ||
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +                (after != 0U) || (spe != 0U) || (sd != SPI_BM_OK))
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: TXPIE = 1 by hand with SPE = 0 (TXP is 1 at reset, 53:3597): the IRQ
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +       fires although no transfer runs. */
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    s_it_spurious = 0U;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IER = SPI_IER_TXPIE;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(1U);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t spurious = s_it_spurious;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    HAL_NVIC_DisableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T6 SPI6 by interrupt (8/16 bits, 1, FIFO + 1, 1024 frames): %lu failed; (c) IRQs in the 10 ms "
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            "after each end, SPE = 0: %lu in total (0)", (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned long)after_total);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +    verdict(spurious >= 1U, "T6(c) control: TXPIE = 1 by hand with SPE = 0: %lu spurious IRQ (>= 1)",
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)spurious);
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T7(b): SPI6 with BDMA (dma_hal, plan D7), buffers in .d3_ram ----------------- */
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +static DMA_HandleTypeDef s_hbd_rx;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +static DMA_HandleTypeDef s_hbd_tx;
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.892s] [host] diff| +static bool bd_init(DMA_HandleTypeDef *h, BDMA_Channel_TypeDef *ch, uint32_t req, uint32_t dir, uint32_t es)
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const DmaHal_Config_t c = {
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        .instance = ch, .request = req, .direction = dir, .periph_inc = DMA_PINC_DISABLE, .mem_inc = DMA_MINC_ENABLE,
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        .periph_align = (es == 1U) ? DMA_PDATAALIGN_BYTE : DMA_PDATAALIGN_HALFWORD,
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        .mem_align = (es == 1U) ? DMA_MDATAALIGN_BYTE : DMA_MDATAALIGN_HALFWORD, .mode = DMA_NORMAL,
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        .priority = DMA_PRIORITY_HIGH, .fifo_mode = DMA_FIFOMODE_DISABLE, .fifo_threshold = DMA_FIFO_THRESHOLD_FULL,
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        .mem_burst = DMA_MBURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_PBURST_SINGLE, .enable_clock = true, .irq_priority = -1 };
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    return DMA_HAL_Init(h, &c) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +/* One SPI6 transfer by BDMA, in the order of plan rule 8 and 53:1262-1274. Returns
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +   WaitEnd's status; *sentinel = RX words still holding the sentinel. */
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t t7b_one(uint32_t bits, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, bool rxdma, uint32_t *mism, uint32_t *crc,
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +                              uint32_t *sentinel, bool *steps_ok)
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 2U;                      /* /8: 12.5 MHz */
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_bd_tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill_const(s_bd_rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sp = SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI6, n, rxdma);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const bool d_ok = bd_init(&s_hbd_rx, BDMA_Channel0, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_RX, DMA_PERIPH_TO_MEMORY, es) &&
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +                      bd_init(&s_hbd_tx, BDMA_Channel1, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_TX, DMA_MEMORY_TO_PERIPH, es);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    bool s_ok = d_ok;
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    if (rxdma && d_ok)
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        s_ok = HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_rx, (uint32_t)&SPI6->RXDR, (uint32_t)s_bd_rx, n) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    if (s_ok)
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        s_ok = HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_tx, (uint32_t)s_bd_tx, (uint32_t)&SPI6->TXDR, n) == HAL_OK;
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t ss = SPI_BM_DmaStart(SPI6);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sw = SPI_BM_WaitEnd(SPI6, budget_of(SPI6, mbr, bits, n));
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    if (rxdma)
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +        (void)HAL_DMA_PollForTransfer(&s_hbd_rx, HAL_DMA_FULL_TRANSFER, 10U);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_tx);                   /* 1. the caller's streams */
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t se = SPI_BM_DmaEnd(SPI6, guard_of(SPI6, mbr));   /* 2. SPI, 3. DMA bits */
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    *mism = sst_mismatch(s_bd_tx, s_bd_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    *crc = sst_crc32_frames(s_bd_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.894s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        if (sst_get(s_bd_rx, es, i) == (0xA5A5A5A5UL & sst_mask(es * 8U)))
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            left++;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    *sentinel = left;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    *steps_ok = (si == SPI_BM_OK) && (sp == SPI_BM_OK) && s_ok && (ss == SPI_BM_OK) && (se == SPI_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                (sd == SPI_BM_OK);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    return sw;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +static void t7b_spi6(void)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t frames[4] = { 1U, 9U, 255U, BD_MAXF };
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 4U; k++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t crc = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            bool steps = false;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one(bits, frames[k], seed, true, &mism, &crc, &left, &steps);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T7B SPI6 b%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%u mis=%lu crc=%08lX", (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)frames[k],
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)mism, (unsigned long)crc);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (mism != 0U) || !steps)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t crc = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    bool steps = false;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one(8U, 255U, next_seed(), false, &mism, &crc, &left, &steps);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T7(b) SPI6 by BDMA (channels 0/1, DMAMUX2 11/12, buffers in .d3_ram), 8/16 bits, 1..4096 "
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            "frames: %lu failed", (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st == SPI_BM_ERR_OVERRUN) && (left == 255U) && steps,
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            "T7(b) control RXDMAEN = 0, 255 frames: WaitEnd %u (ERR_OVERRUN %u), RX words with the sentinel %lu (255)",
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)st, (unsigned)SPI_BM_ERR_OVERRUN, (unsigned long)left);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T8: speed sweep on J1 (pins at VERY_HIGH) ---------------------------------------- */
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +static void t8_sweep(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name, bool m4, uint32_t wide)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pass_mask = 0U;     /* bit mbr = both widths passed */
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad_req = 0U;
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        (void)spi6_pins(0U, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH);
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 8U; i++)
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.896s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t mbr = 7U - i;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t f = kernel_of(spi) >> (mbr + 1U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        bool both = true;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : wide;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            SstXferRes_t r;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            if (m4)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, bits, 0U, 1U, mbr, 0U), 1024U, seed, SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            else
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                xfer_local(&c, 1024U, seed, s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            const bool ok = xfer_ok(&r);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            both = both && ok;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T8 %s /%lu %lu Hz b%lu s=%08lX st=%lu mis=%lu crc=%08lX %s", name, (unsigned long)(2UL << mbr),
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)bits, (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned long)r.status,
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r.mism, (unsigned long)r.crc, (f <= 25000000U) ? "required" : "recorded");
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        if (both)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            pass_mask |= 1UL << mbr;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        else if (f <= 25000000U)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            bad_req |= 1UL << mbr;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t top = 8U;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mbr = 0U; mbr < 8U; mbr++)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        if ((pass_mask & (1UL << mbr)) != 0U)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            top = mbr;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    /* The control at the fastest passing speed: MISO on PB4 (pull-up): must fail. */
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    SstXferRes_t r = { 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    if (top < 8U)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        if (m4)
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_PB4_UP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            (void)xfer_m4(SST_XCFG(0U, 8U, 0U, 1U, top, 0U), 1024U, next_seed(), SST_XM_POLL, &r);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            (void)cm4_send4(SST_CMD_J1PINS, SST_J1P_LOOP, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH, 0U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            (void)spi6_pins(0U, 1U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_VERY_HIGH);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(spi, top, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            xfer_local(&c, 1024U, next_seed(), s_tx, s_rx, &r);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            (void)spi6_pins(0U, 0U, GPIO_BM_SPEED_HIGH);
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad_req == 0U, "T8 %s 1024 frames of 8 and %lu bits, MBR /256../2: passed at /%lu and slower (mask 0x%02lX); "
[+   1.897s] [host] diff| +            "failed where required (<= 25 MHz) 0x%02lX", name, (unsigned long)wide,
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((top < 8U) ? (2UL << top) : 0UL), (unsigned long)pass_mask, (unsigned long)bad_req);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    verdict((top < 8U) && (r.mism > 0U), "T8 %s control at /%lu, MISO on PB4 (pull-up): %lu mismatches (> 0)", name,
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((top < 8U) ? (2UL << top) : 0UL), (unsigned long)r.mism);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T12(a): ES0445 2.22.1 on SPI6 ------------------------------------------------------ */
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +static void t12a_spi6(void)
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 7U;                      /* /256 */
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t g = guard_of(SPI6, mbr);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bud = budget_of(SPI6, mbr, 8U, 1U);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t timeouts = 0U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t data_bad = 0U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t *tx = (uint8_t *)s_tx;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t *rx = (uint8_t *)s_rx;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 10000U; i++)
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        tx[0] = (uint8_t)(i * 37U + 11U);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        rx[0] = (uint8_t)~tx[0];
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI6, tx, rx, 1U, g, bud);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        if (st == SPI_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT)
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            timeouts++;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (rx[0] != tx[0]))
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            data_bad++;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    /* The control (decision 6), by hand: CSTART again with SPE = 1 right after each EOT. */
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR2 = 1U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    /* A stall is waited for 20 frame times, then the SPI is restarted (SPE 0 -> 1);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +       after 20 stalls the control stops: it has shown what it records. */
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t stalls = 0U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t runs = 0U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < 10000U) && (stalls < 20U); i++)
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        *(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)SPI6 + 0x20U) = (uint8_t)i;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_CSTART;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        while (((SPI6->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (20U * g * 10U)))
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        if ((SPI6->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U)
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            stalls++;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->IFCR = SST_SPI_IFCR_ALL;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR2 = 1U;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            (void)*(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)SPI6 + 0x30U);
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +            SPI6->IFCR = SPI_IFCR_EOTC | SPI_IFCR_TXTFC;
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.899s] [host] diff| +        runs++;
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IER = 0U;
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    SPI6->IFCR = SST_SPI_IFCR_ALL;
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI6);
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    verdict((timeouts == 0U) && (data_bad == 0U), "T12(a) 10000 transfers of 1 frame on SPI6 at /256 without pause: "
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +            "%lu ERR_TIMEOUT, %lu wrong (0, 0)", (unsigned long)timeouts, (unsigned long)data_bad);
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +    log_long("T12(a) control by hand (CSTART again with SPE = 1 right after EOT): %lu transfers, %lu stalls; %s "
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +             "(decision 6: recorded only)", (unsigned long)runs, (unsigned long)stalls,
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +             (stalls > 0U) ? "reproduced" : "not reproduced");
[+   1.901s] [host] diff| +}
