[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| + * F1 on the AD2 bus: SPI4 (CPU1, AF5 on PE11..PE14, S13), the AD2 only listening
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +/* PE11 NSS pulled to its inactive level, PE12 SCK to CPOL, PE13 MISO (input) to
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +   `miso_pull`, PE14 MOSI down; each claim reads SPI4 back at rest (S13). */
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +static bool spi4_pins(uint32_t cpol, bool ssiop, uint32_t miso_pull, uint32_t sck_pull)
[+   1.904s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.905s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = GPIO_BM_SPEED_MEDIUM;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const bool ok =
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_NSS, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI4, ssiop ? GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN : GPIO_BM_PULL_UP, 0U) &&
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_SCK, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI4,
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +                (sck_pull != 0xFFU) ? sck_pull : ((cpol != 0U) ? GPIO_BM_PULL_UP : GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN), 0U) &&
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_MISO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI4, miso_pull, 0U) &&
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_MOSI, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_SPI4, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    s_af_speed = GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +/* A handshake whose action runs between the host's HOST_REQ rise (its LA armed) and
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +   MCU_ACK falling. */
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +typedef SpiBm_Status_t (*CapFn_t)(void);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static bool sync_run(CapFn_t fn, SpiBm_Status_t *st)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    if (s_bench_abort)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    ack(true);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = wait_req(true, SYNC_TIMEOUT_CYC);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    if (ok)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        *st = fn();
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    ack(false);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    if (ok)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        ok = wait_req(false, SYNC_TIMEOUT_CYC);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("host timeout");
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Config_t s_cap_cfg;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t       s_cap_n;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t       s_cap_k;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t       s_cap_bad;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t cap_poll(void)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    return SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI4, s_tx, NULL, s_cap_n, guard_of(SPI4, s_cap_cfg.mbr),
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +                           budget_of(SPI4, s_cap_cfg.mbr, s_cap_cfg.data_bits, s_cap_n));
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t cap_dma(void)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t s = SPI_BM_DmaStart(SPI4);
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t w = SPI_BM_WaitEnd(SPI4, budget_of(SPI4, s_cap_cfg.mbr, s_cap_cfg.data_bits, s_cap_n));
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    return (s != SPI_BM_OK) ? s : w;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +/* T12(b)'s control (decision 6), by hand: SPE = 0 right after EOT, no guard. */
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t cap_noguard(void)
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    SPI4->CR2 = 1U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    SPI4->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    *(volatile uint8_t *)((uintptr_t)SPI4 + 0x20U) = (uint8_t)s_tx[0];
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    SPI4->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_CSTART;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    while (((SPI4->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) == 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (20U * CYC_PER_MS)))
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    const bool eot = (SPI4->SR & SPI_SR_EOT) != 0U;
[+   1.906s] [host] diff| +    SPI4->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +    SPI4->IFCR = SST_SPI_IFCR_ALL;
[+   1.907s] [host] diff| +    return eot ? SPI_BM_OK : SPI_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +/* One capture: configuration announced ("CAP"), the transfer inside the handshake,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +   its status afterwards ("CAPR"). The host decodes the LA capture against the seed. */
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +static void cap_one(char kind, const SpiBm_Config_t *c, uint32_t n, CapFn_t fn)
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    if (s_bench_abort)
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    s_cap_cfg = *c;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    s_cap_n = n;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_tx, sst_elem_bytes(c->data_bits), n, c->data_bits, seed);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(c);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    if (!spi4_pins(c->mode >> 1, c->nss_active_high, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0xFFU))
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    if (fn == cap_dma)
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t req = 0U;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_GetDmaRequest(SPI4, true, &req);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        const DmaBm_ConfigEx_t d = {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            .base = { .stream = DMA1_Stream0, .request_id = req, .direction = DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .periph_data_size = (c->data_bits <= 8U) ? DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE : DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_HALFWORD,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .mem_data_size = (c->data_bits <= 8U) ? DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE : DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_HALFWORD,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .periph_inc = false, .mem_inc = true, .circular = false, .priority = DMA_BM_PRIORITY_HIGH,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .enable_it_ht = false, .enable_it_tc = false, .enable_it_te = false, .enable_it_dme = false,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .enable_it_fe = false, .enable_trbuff = false, .enable_fifo = false,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .fifo_threshold = DMA_BM_FIFO_TH_1_2, .periph_addr = (uint32_t)&SPI4->TXDR,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +                      .mem_addr = (uint32_t)s_dma1_buf, .buffer_length = n },
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            .mem1_addr = 0U, .mem_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            .periph_flow_ctrl = false, .fail_if_busy = true, .stop_spin_limit = 0U };
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        sst_fill(s_dma1_buf, sst_elem_bytes(c->data_bits), n, c->data_bits, seed);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        if ((SPI_BM_DmaPrepare(SPI4, n, false) != SPI_BM_OK) || !DMA_BM_InitStreamEx(&d) || !DMA_BM_StartEx(DMA1_Stream0))
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +            s_cap_bad++;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    log_long("CAP %c k%lu m%lu b%lu l%u n%lu nss%lu op%u mssi%lu midi%lu mbr%lu s=%08lX", kind, (unsigned long)s_cap_k,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)c->mode, (unsigned long)c->data_bits, (unsigned)c->lsb_first, (unsigned long)n,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)c->nss, (unsigned)c->nss_active_high, (unsigned long)c->mssi, (unsigned long)c->midi,
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)c->mbr, (unsigned long)seed);
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    if (!sync_run(fn, &st))
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.908s] [host] diff| +    if (fn == cap_dma)
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        (void)DMA_BM_StopEx(DMA1_Stream0, 0U);
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        if (SPI_BM_DmaEnd(SPI4, guard_of(SPI4, c->mbr)) != SPI_BM_OK)
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +            s_cap_bad++;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        (void)DMA_BM_DeinitStream(DMA1_Stream0);
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI4);
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    LOG("CAPR k%lu st=%u", (unsigned long)s_cap_k, (unsigned)st);
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    if ((si != SPI_BM_OK) || (st != SPI_BM_OK) || (sd != SPI_BM_OK))
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        s_cap_bad++;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    s_cap_k++;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +/* Frames that fit in the AD2's 4096 samples at 100 MS/s (one sample per kernel tick at
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +   100 MHz): about 3000 samples of bus time after a 300-sample prefill. */
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t cap_frames(uint32_t bits, uint32_t mbr, uint32_t mssi, uint32_t midi, uint32_t cap)
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t per = 2UL << mbr;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t frame = (bits + midi) * per;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t room = 3000U - ((mssi + 2U) * per);
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = room / frame;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    if (n > cap)
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +        n = cap;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +    return (n < 1U) ? 1U : n;
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.910s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +static void step_spi4_ad2(void)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("SPI4_AD2") || !buschk())
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const bool clk_was_on = spi4_clocked();
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(SPI4);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI4);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    /* MCO1 = HSE/8 on PA8 (AF0) for T5(g); RCC_CFGR saved, restored with read-back (R4). */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cfgr_before = RCC->CFGR;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    s_mco_saved = cfgr_before & (RCC_CFGR_MCO1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    s_mco_changed = true;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    MODIFY_REG(RCC->CFGR, RCC_CFGR_MCO1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE, RCC_CFGR_MCO1_1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE_3);
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (!pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_MCO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, SST_AF_MCO1, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U))
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    /* S5 with PE11..PE14 in AF5 and SPI4 at its reset values (S13). */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    if (!spi4_pins(0U, false, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0xFFU) || !buschk_af(PE_SPI4_MASK, NULL, NULL))
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    s_cap_k = 0U;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    s_cap_bad = 0U;
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    /* (a) modes x {4, 8, 13, 16} x MSB/LSB, hardware SS, /16 */
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ab[4] = { 4U, 8U, 13U, 16U };
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode <= 3U; mode++)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < 4U; ib++)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t lsb = 0U; lsb < 2U; lsb++)
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.911s] [host] diff| +                const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, mode, ab[ib], lsb != 0U, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                           false, 0U, 0U, false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                cap_one('a', &c, cap_frames(ab[ib], 3U, 0U, 0U, 32U), cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* (b) SSIOP = 1 (SSIOP = 0 is (a)) */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW, true, 0U, 0U, false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('b', &c, cap_frames(8U, 3U, 0U, 0U, 16U), cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* (c) SS pulsed between frames: NSS_HW_PULSE with MIDI = 2 */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW_PULSE, false, 0U, 2U,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('c', &c, cap_frames(8U, 3U, 0U, 2U, 12U), cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* (d) MSSI and MIDI 0, 1, 4, 15 */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t dk[4] = { 0U, 1U, 4U, 15U };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW, false, dk[i], 0U,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('d', &c, cap_frames(8U, 3U, dk[i], 0U, 6U), cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW, false, 0U, dk[i],
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('d', &c, cap_frames(8U, 3U, 0U, dk[i], 6U), cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* (g) SCK frequency against MCO1, MBR /4 ... /256; long enough to fill the capture */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mbr = 1U; mbr <= 7U; mbr++)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, mbr, 0U, 16U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT, false, 0U, 0U,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('g', &c, (4000U / (16U * (2U << mbr))) + 2U, cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* T7(c): SPI4 transmit only by DMA1 stream 0 */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW, false, 0U, 0U,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('m', &c, cap_frames(bits, 3U, 0U, 0U, 16U), cap_dma);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* T12(b): CPHA = 0, /256, one frame: the driver's guard, then the control without it */
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT, false, 0U, 0U,
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +                                   false };
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('G', &c, 1U, cap_poll);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +        cap_one('N', &c, 1U, cap_noguard);
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.912s] [host] diff| +    /* (e) keep_pins 1 and 0, SCK pulled up against CPOL = 0, the level read between two
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +       transfers; a keep_pins step ends with Deinit (S13) */
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t keep = 0U; (keep < 2U) && !s_bench_abort; keep++)
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        const bool kp = (keep == 0U);            /* 1 first, then the control 0 */
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        SpiBm_Config_t c = { SPI4, 3U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY, 1U, SPI_BM_NSS_HW, false, 0U, 0U, false };
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        if (!spi4_pins(0U, false, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, GPIO_BM_PULL_UP))
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        c.keep_pins = kp;
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        sst_fill(s_tx, 1U, 2U, 8U, next_seed());
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t s1 = SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI4, s_tx, NULL, 2U, guard_of(SPI4, 3U), budget_of(SPI4, 3U, 8U, 2U));
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        LOG("CAP e k%lu keep%u", (unsigned long)s_cap_k, (unsigned)kp);
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())                              /* host: DIO4 level between the transfers */
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t s2 = SPI_BM_Transfer(SPI4, s_tx, NULL, 2U, guard_of(SPI4, 3U), budget_of(SPI4, 3U, 8U, 2U));
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_BM_Deinit(SPI4);
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        if ((si != SPI_BM_OK) || (s1 != SPI_BM_OK) || (s2 != SPI_BM_OK) || (sd != SPI_BM_OK))
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +            s_cap_bad++;
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +        s_cap_k++;
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +    LOG("CAP done k%lu", (unsigned long)s_cap_k);
[+   1.914s] [host] diff| +    verdict(s_cap_bad == 0U, "T5/T7(c)/T12(b) SPI4: %lu captures and level reads run, %lu with a failed Init, transfer, "

[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    """Frame i = the (i + 1)-th output of xorshift32 (13, 17, 5) from `seed`, masked."""
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    x, out, m = seed & 0xFFFFFFFF, [], mask(bits)

[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    for v in vals:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        ones |= v
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        zeros |= ~v & mask(bits)
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    return ones & zeros
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +def calc_prescaler(k: int, x: int) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    """Plan rule: the smallest MBR with k / 2^(MBR+1) <= x; SCK = floor(k / 2^(MBR+1))."""
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    if k == 0 or x == 0:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        return ST_PARAM, 0, 0
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    for m in range(8):
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        if k <= x * (1 << (m + 1)):
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +            return ST_OK, m, k >> (m + 1)
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    return ST_RANGE, 0, 0
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +def calc_guard(c: int, k: int, m: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    """One SCK period in CPU cycles, rounded up: ceil(c * 2^(m+1) / k)."""
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    if c == 0 or k == 0 or m > 7:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        return ST_PARAM, 0
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    v = -(-(c << (m + 1)) // k)
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    return (ST_RANGE, 0) if v > 0xFFFFFFFF else (ST_OK, v)
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +def joined(lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    """Console lines with log_long()'s continuation pieces ("[spi] | ...") put back
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    on the line they continue."""
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    out: list[str] = []
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    for line in lines:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        i = line.find("[spi] | ")
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        if i >= 0 and out:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +            out[-1] += " " + line[i + len("[spi] | "):]
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +        else:
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +            out.append(line)
[+   1.933s] [host] diff| +    return out

[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +def la_segments(samples: list[int], bit: int, level: int) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    """The runs of `bit` at `level` (0 or 1) in LA samples, as (first, last + 1)
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    sample indices; a run cut by either end of the capture is included."""
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    runs: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    start = None
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    for i, s in enumerate(samples):
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +        on = ((s >> bit) & 1) == level
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +        if on and start is None:
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +            start = i
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +        elif not on and start is not None:
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +            runs.append((start, i))
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +            start = None
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    if start is not None:
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +        runs.append((start, len(samples)))
[+   1.956s] [host] diff| +    return runs

[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +def spi_sample_edges(samples: list[int], sck_bit: int, cpol: int, cpha: int) -> list[int]:
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    """Sample indices of the SCK edges on which an SPI receiver samples data
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    (Motorola format): the leading edge for CPHA = 0, the trailing edge for
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    CPHA = 1; leading is rising for CPOL = 0 and falling for CPOL = 1. So the
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    sampling edge rises when CPOL == CPHA and falls otherwise."""
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    rises, falls = la_edges(samples, sck_bit)
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    return rises if (cpol ^ cpha) == 0 else falls
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +def spi_decode(samples: list[int], sck_bit: int, data_bit: int, cpol: int, cpha: int, bits: int,
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +               lsb_first: bool) -> tuple[list[int], int]:
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    """Frames of `bits` bits read from `data_bit` at every sampling edge of SCK
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    (spi_sample_edges), MSB or LSB first. Returns (frames, number of sampling
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    edges); a trailing partial frame is not returned. Only listens: works on a
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    capture already made."""
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    edges = spi_sample_edges(samples, sck_bit, cpol, cpha)
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    vals = [(samples[i] >> data_bit) & 1 for i in edges]
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    frames = []
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    for f in range(len(vals) // bits):
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +        chunk = vals[f * bits:(f + 1) * bits]
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +        if lsb_first:
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +            chunk = chunk[::-1]
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +        v = 0
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +        for b in chunk:
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +            v = (v << 1) | b
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +        frames.append(v)
[+   1.957s] [host] diff| +    return frames, len(edges)
